SB6 builder bank: EVERY word x frame line, every card combination (builder-check v1)
One block per word: its frames, then every sentence of each frame with the word in {w}; then the model.

## the   [o020 o021]
 o020 {w} {a} is {b}.
    The dog is big.
    The dog is new.
    The dog is nice.
    The cake is big.
    The cake is new.
    The cake is nice.
    The kite is big.
    The kite is new.
    The kite is nice.
 o021 I can see {w} {a} {b}.
    I can see the dog here.
    I can see the dog there.
    I can see the dog outside.
    I can see the cat here.
    I can see the cat there.
    I can see the cat outside.
    I can see the kite here.
    I can see the kite there.
    I can see the kite outside.
 model: We saw the moon last night.

    A kite is big.

## is   [v020 v021]

    The dog is very nice.
    The cake is very big.
    The cake is very new.
    The cake is very nice.
    The kite is very big.

    The kite is very nice.
 v021 My {a} {w} in the {b}.

    My cat is in the car.
    My book is in the bag.
    My book is in the box.

    My hat is in the bag.

    My hat is in the car.
